# Agents · S · part 10

1,009 entries, seat-clerk to security. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **seat-clerk**: A seat's bookkeeper. Hire it when your inbox is long (more rows than you can act on in one pass), before a handoff or compaction, or after join lists memory files to triage. Given a seat id, it reads the seat's inbox, handoff and notes from GitHub and returns one line per row with the act it needs, draft acks, a draft handoff text and a memory-audit triage. It drafts only; it never posts an ack, a close, an answer, a status or notes, so the seat stays the one that writes. ([DeSciX/daita-beast](https://github.com/DeSciX/daita-beast/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/seat-clerk.md))

- **secAgent**: Use quando Felipe pedir "executar a varredura de segurança". Varre código e documentos arquiteturais em busca de falhas de segurança, produzindo um relatório de issues encontradas ordenado por severidade. Só cria issues no GitHub após aprovação explícita. ([felipe-japor/cashflow-platform](https://github.com/felipe-japor/cashflow-platform/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/secAgent.md))
- **secaudit-verifier**: Adversarial verifier for security findings. Given a candidate vulnerability (with its evidence and repro), it tries to REFUTE it — checking reachability, exploitability, whether the flagged version is actually affected, and whether a control elsewhere mitigates it. Use to triage high/critical findings before they go in the report, and to filter scanner false positives. ([mtvrkan/secaudit](https://github.com/mtvrkan/secaudit/blob/HEAD/plugins/secaudit/agents/secaudit-verifier.md) · ★2)
- **seccion**: Construye UNA sección aislada en secciones/&lt;slug&gt;/. Trabajo paralelo: no toca nada fuera de su carpeta. ([WalterSolorzano/proj-k7x-assets](https://github.com/WalterSolorzano/proj-k7x-assets/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/seccion.md))
- **secdevops-engineer**: Sichert Pipeline, Secrets und Lieferkette ab, sobald Build, Abhängigkeiten, Schlüsselablage oder der Manipulationsschutz des Protokolls geändert werden. ([valITino/r3cosint](https://github.com/valITino/r3cosint/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/secdevops-engineer.md) · ★1)
- **SECGEN_ADAPTABLE_CATALOGUE**: These SecGen scenarios are live and known-good. A mission's VM **may be rebased on one of these** if it enhances play (user, this pass). Treat a rebase as a design improvement, not a mere consistency fix: it means adapting the chosen scenario's systems/modules into the mission's canonical SecGen XML (`break_escape/safetynet/&lt;mission&gt;.xml`) and re-aligning every game reference (flags, accounts, hos ([cliffe/BreakEscape](https://github.com/cliffe/BreakEscape/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/SECGEN_ADAPTABLE_CATALOGUE.md) · ★6)
- **SECGEN_CLAIMS_CHECK**: Fact check of the game text against the SecGen scenario XML and module metadata. No exploit detail is recorded here. ([cliffe/BreakEscape](https://github.com/cliffe/BreakEscape/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/SECGEN_CLAIMS_CHECK.md) · ★6)
- **SECGEN_MODULE_PLAN**: Written for the author. Goal: stop over-using distcc (currently m03, m04, m06), draw every substitute from the **known-good catalogue scenarios** (so they are Bookworm-tested — shellshock is not), and line up VM module candidates for the unauthored missions (m09, m10). ([cliffe/BreakEscape](https://github.com/cliffe/BreakEscape/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/SECGEN_MODULE_PLAN.md) · ★6)
- **SECGEN_REVIEW_BRIEF**: You are reviewing and fixing one SAFETYNET mission's SecGen/VM integration. Run the `.claude/skills/mission-secgen-review/SKILL.md` skill in full — it is the method. This brief adds the facts already established so you don't re-derive them, and the scope limits for running in parallel with other missions' agents. ([cliffe/BreakEscape](https://github.com/cliffe/BreakEscape/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/SECGEN_REVIEW_BRIEF.md) · ★6)
- **second-avis**: Relecture adversariale en lecture seule d'un diff de story, sur Haiku, pour quelques centimes. À lancer par l'orchestrateur après dev-story et avant reviewer, à qui l'on transmet sa liste. Il trouve des défauts que les autres relecteurs manquent (dépendance absente en CI, branchement oublié). Ne corrige rien, ne décide pas seul. ([Aliquanto3/agent-cost-kit](https://github.com/Aliquanto3/agent-cost-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/second-avis.md) · ★1)
- **second-brain**: Knowledge base management. Use for taking notes, creating and retrieving meeting records, research synthesis, finding what was discussed with a person or on a topic, and keeping the vault healthy (wikilinks, frontmatter, archival). Read-only by default; writes happen on explicit intent. ([chacosoldier/compabob](https://github.com/chacosoldier/compabob/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/second-brain.md) · ★34)
- **second-brain**: Fresh perspective agent for challenging ideas, decisions, and solutions. Use when you want a second opinion that thinks deeply, researches thoroughly, and pushes back on surface-level thinking. ([samyak163/Kalasetu-project](https://github.com/samyak163/Kalasetu-project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/second-brain.md) · ★1)
- **second-brain-curator**: Maintains the user's Obsidian "second brain" vault (Karpathy LLM-Wiki pattern) and its ingest pipeline — runs/debugs the transcript exporter and headless ingest, seeds or edits wiki pages, lints vault conventions, and answers questions by searching raw/ and wiki/. Use for any task touching &lt;user-notes&gt;/knowledge-vault or ~/.claude/second-brain/. ([UribeLandscapes/claude-agents](https://github.com/UribeLandscapes/claude-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/second-brain-curator.md) · ★2)

- **second-check**: The second look in /skill-health:check. Tries to disprove each failing or deprecated finding in one skill before it is saved. Used by /skill-health:check; not for anything else. ([mvoshell652/skill-health](https://github.com/mvoshell652/skill-health/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/second-check.md))
- **second-checker**: Give this agent a list of items, each with: the manuscript sentence, the claim, the cited key, and the path to `sources/text/&lt;key&gt;.txt`. Do **not** give it the first checker's verdict, quote or reasoning. Output path: `$RV/work/factcheck/second-&lt;n&gt;.json`. ([gauravfs-14/detailed-paper-review](https://github.com/gauravfs-14/detailed-paper-review/blob/HEAD/agents/second-checker.md))
- **second-juge**: Second juge de la cuisine, lancé par la station sur une livraison qui touche à ce qui la juge (`.claude/brigade/`, `.github/workflows/`), une fois le premier reviewer d'accord — il vérifie qu'elle n'affaiblit aucun contrôle et que son ticket la justifie. ([benomite/brigade](https://github.com/benomite/brigade/blob/HEAD/agents/second-juge.md) · ★3)
- **second-opinion**: Adversarial, fresh-context review of the current working-tree diff before wrapping up or committing. Dissects changes for corpus regressions, blast radius, inappropriate helper coupling, and circular tests. Runs read-only checks and corpus diff comparisons against HEAD. ([staffanm/ferenda](https://github.com/staffanm/ferenda/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/second-opinion.md) · ★42)
- **second-opinion**: Читающий-только советчик на самой сильной модели. Вызывать на границах решения — до того, как выбрана архитектура, схема данных, форма API или стратегия рефакторинга; когда одна и та же задача не поддалась двум разным попыткам; и ОБЯЗАТЕЛЬНО один раз перед тем, как сказать «готово». Получает решение (или диф) и названные ограничения, возвращает вердикт: отдавать / сначала починить вот это / передумать. Советует, но никогда не правит. ([edvardgrishin27/superstack-plugin](https://github.com/edvardgrishin27/superstack-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/superstack/agents/second-opinion.md) · ★18)
- **second-opinion**: Independent fresh-context critique. Spawn this agent when you want a take from a Claude that has NOT seen the conversation — useful for sanity-checking a recommendation, reviewing a plan before execution, or breaking out of a reasoning rut. Pass the full problem statement, the proposed approach, and any constraints in the spawn prompt — the agent has zero conversation history. Returns a written critique with agreements, disagreements, missed considerations, and a verdict. ([AlphaBravoCompany/codsworth-marketplace](https://github.com/AlphaBravoCompany/codsworth-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/adhoc/agents/second-opinion.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **second-opinion**: Independent second opinion on any artifact (copy, UI or visual design, software architecture, code, plans, prompts), biased toward the simplest thing that fully does the job. - **second-opinion**: Provides independent second opinions using Kimi k2.5 model. Analyzes code, architecture, and problems from a fresh perspective. ([phsaurav/dotfiles](https://github.com/phsaurav/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.config/opencode/agents/second-opinion.md) · ★2)
- **second-opinion**: Independent second-pass critique of a spec, plan, or diff — a different model's eyes, on demand. Advisory only, never a merge gate. ([dngioidev/forge](https://github.com/dngioidev/forge/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/second-opinion.md) · ★1)

([sdolard/awesome-claude-config](https://github.com/sdolard/awesome-claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/second-opinion.md) · ★1)
- **second-opinion**: 独立したセカンドオピニオンを提供する読み取り専用エージェント。 問題定義・前提・設計案を受け取り、cold-read の構造化された独立見解を返す。 second-opinion スキルのルートA（既定）として、gstack office-hours Phase 3.5 や 汎用的なセカンドオピニオンで使用。codex CLI 検出時に選べるルートB（外部 cold-read）は このエージェントを介さず SKILL.md が直接 `codex exec` を呼ぶ。 使用例: - user: "このアイデアにセカンドオピニオンがほしい" assistant: "second-opinion エージェントで独立した見解を取得します。" 使わない場面: - 会話の文脈を踏まえた技術相談は senpai / senior-engineer に振る。 このエージェントは会話履歴を持たず、渡されたコンテキストだけで判断する cold-read 専用。 - 実装の是非を指摘してほしい場合は tacchi（実態突合・過剰約束の検出）に振る。 ([todoroki-godai/evolve-anything](https://github.com/todoroki-godai/evolve-anything/blob/HEAD/agents/second-opinion.md) · ★1)
- **second-opinion**: Invoke only after the `stuck-recovery` skill has already been loaded and at least one deliberate strategy switch has also failed, or after three or more distinct hypotheses have been tested against the same symptom without resolution. The fresh context is the point — this agent should not be briefed extensively on what's been tried, only on what the actual current problem is. Do not invoke for code review, design feedback, validating a working solution, or as a reflexive "second pair of eyes" on a normal PR. ([albeorla/complexity-awareness](https://github.com/albeorla/complexity-awareness/blob/HEAD/agents/second-opinion.md))

- **second-opinion**: Fresh-context UI reviewer for Via. Sees only screenshots and a one-line brief, never the code. Use only from /via audit and /via check, when via_second_opinion.py exits 10. ([ctorres345/NousFX](https://github.com/ctorres345/NousFX/blob/HEAD/plugins/via/agents/second-opinion.md))

- **second-order**: Read-only consequence auditor. Give it ONE named change — proposed or just-landed — and it asks "and then what?" across five failure shapes drawn from real incidents in this repo (propagation, consumers, latency, evidence class, lifecycle), plus an inversion pass. Returns a punch list with a concrete artifact behind every finding, or "nothing found." Call it before declaring a fix done, before moving files, and before speaking a count.
